--- PAGE 156 ---
2 
 
Attachment 4 
to the DCRT Large Load Interconnection Process and Timeline  
DCRT TO Tariff, Appendix IV 
INTERCONNECTION FINANCIAL SECURITY 
Defined Terms: 
 
Construction Activities shall mean any activities related to the construction of the Network 
Upgrades, including but not limited to equipment procurement, engineering, construction and 
associated activities. 
 
Current Cost Responsibility shall mean the Large Load Interconnection Customer’s current 
allocated costs for Network Upgrades, not to exceed the Maximum Cost Responsibility, in each 
case pursuant to Section 4 of the Facilities Study Agreement. This cost is used to calculate the 
Large Load Interconnection Customer’s Interconnection Financial Security requirement. 
 
Maximum Cost Responsibility (MCR) shall mean, pursuant to this Attachment 4 and the 
Facilities Study Agreement, the full cost of: (1) assigned Network Upgrades and (2) Participating 
Transmission Operator’s (TO) Interconnection Facilities. 
 
1. Types of Interconnection Financial Security. 
The Interconnection Financial Security posted by a Large Load Interconnection Customer 
may be any combination of the following types of Interconnection Financial Security 
provided in favor of the Participating TO: 
 
(a) 
an irrevocable and unconditional letter of credit issued by a bank or financial 
institution that has a credit rating of A or better by Standard and Poors or A2 
or better by Moody’s; 
 
(b) 
an irrevocable and unconditional surety bond issued by an insurance company 
that has a credit rating of A or better by Standard and Poors or A2 or better by 
Moody’s; 
 
(c) 
an unconditional and irrevocable guaranty issued by a company that has a 
credit rating of A or better by Standard and Poors or A2 or better by Moody’s;  
 
(d) 
a cash deposit standing to the credit of the Participating TO in an interest-
bearing escrow account maintained at a bank or financial institution that is 
reasonably acceptable to the Participating TO; 
 
(e) 
a certificate of deposit in the name of the Participating TO issued by a bank or 
financial institution that has a credit rating of A or better by Standard and 
Poors or A2 or better by Moody’s; or
--- PAGE 157 ---
3 
 
(f) 
a payment bond certificate in the name of the Participating TO issued by a 
bank or financial institution that has a credit rating of A or better by Standard 
and Poors or A2 or better by Moody’s. 
 
Interconnection Financial Security instruments as listed above shall be in such form as 
the Participating TO may reasonably require from time to time by notice to Large Load 
Interconnection Customers or in such other form as has been evaluated and approved as 
reasonably acceptable by the Participating TO. 
 
The Participating TO shall publish and maintain standardized forms related to the types 
of Interconnection Financial Security listed above which shall be accessible on the 
Participating TO’S Website. The Participating TO shall require the use of standardized 
forms of Interconnection Financial Security to the greatest extent possible. If at any time 
the guarantor of the Interconnection Financial Security fails to maintain the credit rating 
required by this Section 1, the Large Load Interconnection Customer shall provide to the 
Participating TO replacement Interconnection Financial Security meeting the 
requirements of this Section 1 within five (5) Business Days of the change in credit 
rating. 
 
Interest on a cash deposit standing to the credit of the Participating TO in an interest-
bearing escrow account under Section 1(d) will accrue to the account of the Large Load 
Interconnection Customer. 
 
2. Interconnection Financial Security-Initial Posting for Large Load Interconnection 
Customers. 
2.1 Each Large Load Interconnection Customer shall post, with notice to the 
Participating TO, two (2) separate Interconnection Financial Security instruments: 
(i) a posting relating to the applicable Network Upgrades; and (ii) a posting 
relating to the Participating TO’s Interconnection Facilities. 
 
2.2 Timing of Postings. The postings set forth in this Section 2 shall be made any 
time after the issuance of the Facilities Study report but no later than ninety (90) 
calendar days after issuance of the Facilities Study report for Large Load 
Interconnection. 
 
2.3 Revised Study Reports. If the Participating TO revises a Facilities Study, the 
initial postings will be due from the Large Load Interconnection Customer by the 
later of ninety (90) calendar days after issuance of the original Facilities Study 
Report or forty (40) calendar days after issuance of the revised Facilities Study 
report.
--- PAGE 158 ---
4 
 
2.4 Posting Amount for Network Upgrades. 
 
2.4.1 Large Load Interconnection Customers. Each Large Load 
Interconnection Customer for a Large Load Facility shall post an 
Interconnection Financial Security instrument relating to the Network 
Upgrades assigned to such Large Load Interconnection Customer pursuant 
to the Facilities Study. 
The posting amount for such Network Upgrades shall equal the lesser of: 
(i) fifteen percent (15%) of the Current Cost Responsibility assigned to the 
Large Load Interconnection Customer in the Facilities Study for Network 
Upgrades; (ii) $20,000 per megawatt of electrical load of the Large Load 
Facility or the amount of megawatt increase in the load of each existing 
Large Load Facility as listed by the Large Load Interconnection Customer 
in its Large Load Interconnection Application, including any requested 
modifications thereto; or (iii) $7,500,000, but in no event less than 
$500,000. 
 
2.5 Posting Amount for Participating TO Interconnection Facilities. 
2.5.1 Large Load Interconnection Customers. Each Large Load 
Interconnection Customer for a Large Load Facility shall post an 
Interconnection Financial Security instrument in an amount equal to the 
lesser of: (i) fifteen percent (15%) of the Current Cost Responsibility 
assigned to the Large Load Interconnection Customer in the Facilities 
Study for Participating TO’s Interconnection Facilities; (ii) $20,000 per 
megawatt of electrical load of the Large Load Facility or the amount of 
megawatt increase in the load of each existing Large Load Facility as 
listed by the Large Load Interconnection Customer in its Large Load 
Interconnection Application including any requested modifications 
thereto; or (iii) $7,500,000, but in no event less than $500,000. 
2.6 Cost Estimates Less than Minimum Posting Amounts. If (1) the Current Cost 
Responsibility of the assigned Network Upgrades, (2) the allocated costs of the 
Participating TO Interconnection Facilities, or (3) both are less than the respective 
minimum posting amounts that would apply under Sections 2.3.1 or 2.4.1, then 
the posting amount required will equal the Current Cost Responsibility of the 
Assigned Network Upgrades or the allocated costs for Participating TO 
Interconnection Facilities, as applicable. 
 
2.7 Consequences for Failure to Post. The failure by a Large Load Interconnection 
Customer to timely post the Interconnection Financial Security required by this 
Attachment 4 shall result in the Large Load Interconnection Application being 
deemed withdrawn. The Large Load Interconnection Customer shall provide the 
Participating TO and the Participating TO with written notice that it has posted 
the required Interconnection Financial Security no later than the applicable final 
day for posting. The failure by a Large Load Interconnection Customer to timely
--- PAGE 159 ---
5 
 
post the Interconnection Financial Security required by this Section 2 shall result 
in the Large Load Interconnection Application being deemed withdrawn. The 
Large Load Interconnection Customer shall provide the Participating TO with 
written notice that it has posted the required Interconnection Financial Security no 
later than the applicable final day for posting. 
 
2.8 Re-calculation of Initial Posting Requirement. If withdrawals, modifications, 
or system changes occur after the completion of the Facilities Study, and the 
Participating TO, is able to reasonably determine, prior to the date for initial 
posting of Interconnection Financial Security, that as a result of such decrease 
(solely or in combination with other modifications made by Large Load 
Interconnection Customers) some of the Network Upgrades and/or Participating 
TO Interconnection Facilities identified in the Facilities Study will no longer be 
required, then the calculation of the initial posting of Interconnection Financial 
Security will not include those Network Upgrades and/or Participating TO 
Interconnection Facilities. Such determination will be made based on the 
Participating TO’s best engineering judgment and will not include any re-studies.  
 
3. Interconnection Financial Security –Second and Third Postings for Large Load 
Interconnection Customers. 
 
3.1 Second Posting for Large Load Interconnection Customers. 
 
3.1.1 Each Large Load Interconnection Customer shall make second postings, 
with notice to the Participating TO, of two separate Interconnection 
Financial Security instruments: (i) a second posting relating to the 
Network Upgrades; and (ii) a second posting relating to the Participating 
TO’s Interconnection Facilities. The Current Cost Responsibility for 
calculating the second and third Interconnection Financial Security 
postings for Large Load Interconnection Customers shall be set forth in 
the Facilities Study report. 
 
3.1.2 The postings set forth in this Section 3 for second postings for Large Load 
Interconnection Customers shall be made any time after issuance of the 
Facilities Study report but no later than thirty (30) calendar days prior to 
the date on which the Facilities Study report provides that such amounts 
will be needed to fund long lead time equipment procurement as part of 
the Construction Activities for the Network Upgrades or the Participating 
TO’s Interconnection Facilities, whichever is earlier. 
 
3.2 Third Posting for Large Load Interconnection Customers. 
 
3.2.1 After the second posting for a Large Load Interconnection Customer has 
been made but no later than thirty (30) calendar days after the date on 
which the Participating TO provides notice that Participating TO is 
obligated to provide security needed to fund any additional obligations for
--- PAGE 160 ---
6 
 
Network Upgrades or Participating TO’s Interconnection Facilities on 
behalf of the Large Load Interconnection Customer, whichever is earlier, 
the Large Load Interconnection Customer shall modify the two (2) 
separate Interconnection Financial Security instruments posted pursuant to 
Section 3.1. 
 
3.3 Determining Refundable Portion of the Interconnection Financial Security 
for Network Upgrades. 
3.3.1 Withdrawal Between the First Posting and the Deadline for the 
Second Posting. If the Large Load Interconnection Customer either 
withdraws its Large Load Interconnection Application or terminates its 
Large Load Interconnection Agreement (LLIA) at any time between the 
initial posting and the deadline for the second posting of the 
Interconnection Financial Security for applicable Network Upgrades, then 
the Participating TO shall liquidate the Interconnection Financial Security 
for the applicable Network Upgrades and reimburse the Large Load 
Interconnection Customer the lesser of: 
a. 
the Interconnection Financial Security plus (any other provided 
security plus any separately provided capital) less (all costs and 
expenses incurred or irrevocably committed to finance Pre-
Construction Activities for Network Upgrades on behalf of the 
Large Load Interconnection Customer); or 
 
b. 
the Interconnection Financial Security plus (any other provided 
security plus any separately provided capital) minus the lesser 
of fifty percent (50%) of the value of the posted 
Interconnection Financial Security for Network Upgrades or 
$10,000 per requested and approved, pre-downsized megawatt 
of the Large Load Facility load. 
 
3.3.2 Withdrawal Between the Second Posting and the Commencement of 
Construction Activities. If the Large Load Interconnection Customer 
either withdraws or terminates its LLIA at any time after the second 
posting of the Interconnection Financial Security for applicable Network 
Upgrades and the Commencement of Construction Activities for such 
Network Upgrades, then the Participating TO shall liquidate the 
Interconnection Financial Security for the applicable Network Upgrades 
and reimburse the Large Load Interconnection Customer the lesser of: 
 
a. 
the Interconnection Financial Security plus (any other provided 
security plus any separately provided capital) less (all costs and 
expenses incurred or irrevocably committed to finance Pre-
Construction Activities for Network Upgrades on behalf of the 
Large Load Interconnection Customer) and less (any posting
--- PAGE 161 ---
7 
 
reduction due to the Large Load Interconnection Customer’s 
election to self-build Stand Alone Network Upgrades); or 
b. 
the Interconnection Financial Security plus (any other provided 
security plus any separately provided capital) minus the lesser 
of fifty percent (50%) of the value of the posted 
Interconnection Financial Security for Network Upgrades or 
$20,000 per requested and approved, pre-downsized megawatt 
of the Large Load Facility load. 
3.3.3 Special Treatment Based on Failure to Obtain Necessary Permit or 
Authorization from Governmental Authority. If, at any time after the 
second posting requirement, the Large Load Interconnection Customer 
withdraws the Large Load Interconnection Application or terminates the 
LLIA, as applicable, because the Large Load Interconnection Customer 
received a final denial from the primary issuing Governmental Authority 
for authorization necessary for the construction or operation of the Large 
Load Facility, and the Network Upgrades to be financed by the Large 
Load Interconnection Customer are also to be financed by one or more 
other Large Load Interconnection Customers, then the provisions of 
Section 3.3.2 apply, except that the Large Load Interconnection Customer 
shall not be reimbursed for its share of any actual costs incurred or 
irrevocably committed by the Participating TO for Construction Activities. 
 
3.3.4 After Commencement of Construction Activities. Once Construction 
Activities on Network Upgrades on behalf of the Large Load 
Interconnection Customer commence, any withdrawal of the Large Load 
Interconnection Application or termination of the LLIA by the Large Load 
Interconnection Customer will be treated as follows: 
 
The Participating TO shall liquidate the Interconnection Financial 
Security, or balance thereof, posted by the Large Load Interconnection 
Customer for Network Upgrades at the time of withdrawal. 
 
To the extent the amount of the liquidated Interconnection Financial 
Security plus capital, if any, separately provided by the Large Load 
Interconnection Customer to satisfy its obligation to finance Network 
Upgrades exceeds the total cost responsibility for Network Upgrades 
assigned to the Large Load Interconnection Customer, the Participating 
TO shall remit to the Large Load Interconnection Customer the excess 
amount. 
 
Withdrawal of a Large Load Interconnection Application or termination of 
a LLIA shall result in the release to the Large Load Interconnection 
Customer of any Interconnection Financial Security posted by the Large 
Load Interconnection Customer for Participating TO Interconnection 
Facilities, except with respect to any amounts necessary to pay for costs
--- PAGE 162 ---
8 
 
incurred or irrevocably committed by the Participating TO on behalf of the 
Large Load Interconnection Customer for the Participating TO’s 
Interconnection Facilities and for which the Participating TO has not been 
reimbursed in accordance with this Attachment 4. Withdrawal of a Large 
Load Interconnection Application or termination of a LLIA shall result in 
the release to the Large Load Interconnection Customer of any 
Interconnection Financial Security posted by the Large Load 
Interconnection Customer for Participating TO Interconnection Facilities, 
except with respect to any amounts necessary to pay for costs incurred or 
irrevocably committed by the Participating TO on behalf of the Large 
Load Interconnection Customer for the Participating TO’s Interconnection 
Facilities and for which the Participating TO has not been reimbursed in 
accordance with this Section 3.3.4. 
 
3.3.5 Accounting by The Participating TO. Within twenty (20) calendar days 
of any liquidating event, the Participating TO shall provide the 
Participating TO and Large Load Interconnection Customer with an 
accounting of the disposition of the proceeds of the liquidated 
Interconnection Financial Security and remit to the Participating TO all 
proceeds not otherwise reimbursed to the Large Load Interconnection 
Customer or applied to costs incurred or irrevocably committed by the 
Participating TO on behalf of the Large Load Interconnection Customer in 
accordance with this Attachment 4.
